# Refactoring inventory

Original inventory: 2026-10-02 at e1eb094; current update 2026-10-04 for E09 from clean checkpoint f06543b. E08 browser completion is user-reported and its local checkpoint verified. Current auth anchors reflect the 1,411-line reduction and new timetable-controller factory; historical execution logs retain stage-specific anchors. HTML/inline locations outside the current contracts manifest retain original snapshot references. No commit, push, deployment or database changes.

## Coverage and interpretation

- Current browser files: app.js (3,698 lines), auth.js (45,925), self-registration-links.js (175), supabase-config.js (43), js/website/why-grid.js (37), js/website/practice-grid.js (21), js/core/escape-html.js (8), js/website/offerings.js (207), js/features/timetable/store.js (591), js/features/students/store.js (314), js/shared-ui/action-dialog.js (202), and js/features/timetable/controller.js (1,488). All twelve parse as classic scripts.
- Original catalog: 1,222 named declarations. Current catalog: 1,228 after E08 and E09 factory/wiring additions: 250 in app.js, 16 in students/store.js, 43 in timetable/store.js, five in offerings.js, two in why-grid.js, one in practice-grid.js, one in escape-html.js, eight across the dialog IIFE/factory, two across the timetable-controller IIFE/factory, 889 inside auth.js, 10 inside the registration-link IIFE and one in configuration. E09 replaces one function with a private alias in auth.js; the moved function retains all nested declarations/state. Const bindings do not make their objects immutable.
- Inspected all 58 root HTML documents, their ordered script tags and three inline scripts. Two inline scripts parse; contact.html does not (see checklist.md). The verification HTML has no scripts.
- Four Supabase function interfaces are retained in place; contracts.md records their request/response shapes. SQL/schema files were inspected for integration context, not run.
- No AGENTS.md was found in the repository (including hidden paths excluding .git) or its ancestor chain. No package.json or test/spec files were found in the source-file scan. Existing system parsers were used; no dependencies installed.
- Dependency/consumer columns use lexical bindings, not a complete runtime call graph. Nested callbacks are attributed to their outer owner. Cross-file window manager access is catalogued separately. Dynamic selectors, computed properties, string references and runtime event dispatch still require feature-specific review. Zero lexical references is NOT permission to delete a symbol.
- Grouping/destinations remain proposals except E01–E09. E09 moves initTimetableControls and its complete nested state/behavior into a private factory, with one auth alias and unchanged page caller. Renderers, helpers, managers, permissions, storage and synchronization stay in place. Row labels distinguish moved code. Stop after E09.
- Default password constants are listed by name only. No configuration keys, account data, or stored user values are copied here.

## Module-scope effects and initialization

E09: controller.js publishes only SchoolSphereTimetableUI.createController at line 1487. auth.js:13697–13731 creates one factory and binds initTimetableControls privately. Factory load/construction does not access DOM/storage or register events. The retained initAdminSchedulePage call at auth.js:42717 creates state and handlers at the original startup point; other pages return before calling it.

E08: action-dialog.js evaluates an IIFE that publishes only SchoolSphereActionDialogs.createController. No DOM/listener/timer work occurs at load or controller creation. auth.js:6312–6320 creates one controller at the former state declaration location; its seven original declarations initialize there, not at script import. The dialog element and three handlers remain lazy.

E07: students/store.js contains only 16 function declarations. It performs no import-time storage, DOM, event, network, listener or timer work and creates no new state. The original app.js adapter still owns initialization. Nested promotion/exam normalizers remain inside normalizeStudentRecord; progression callbacks remain passed by callers.

E06: store.js initializes seven const-held arrays/default catalogs, including the period flatMap derived only from earlier declarations in the same file. These are unfrozen arrays with unchanged identities/semantics, not window properties. Evaluation does not read storage, access the DOM, dispatch events, register listeners or timers, or create the manager. Methods resolve retained app.js helpers/constants only when called.

These execute when their current script/wrapper executes or register later work. Manager assignments must finish before auth consumes them.

### Inline script exceptions

- contact.html:906–967: FAQ click handlers on [data-faq]; contact-form submit validation; 1,200 ms simulated success timer. The apostrophe at line 958 causes a syntax failure for the entire inline script, so these handlers cannot initialize as written. Proposed future owner: js/website/contact.js; retain now and address the defect separately.
- admin-settings.html:10–12: immediate location.replace to ./admin-settings-school.html, alongside a meta refresh. Proposed owner: retain in HTML initially; do not delay this redirect.
- admin-messages.html:593 onward: IIFE returns immediately after use strict. Dormant inbox/notification scanning/rendering and listeners must remain dormant. Proposed disposition: retain; removal requires separate reviewed cleanup.
- self-registration-links.js:170–174: DOM-ready guard; initRegistrationLinkCard schedules refreshLink at 250 and 1,000 ms, and showCopied resets status at 1,800 ms. Overlapping auth.js registration-link controls are a dependency risk, not permission to merge them now.
